{};\",\n            sql_text(&campaign.id)\n        ))?;\n        if !exists.trim().is_empty() {\n            return Ok(false);\n        }\n        self.sql(&format!(\n            \"INSERT INTO campaigns(id,name,dm) VALUES ({},{},{});\",\n            sql_text(&campaign.id),\n            sql_text(&campaign.name),\n            sql_text(&campaign.dm)\n        ))\n        .map(|_| true)\n    }\n\n    fn create_play_campaign(&self, campaign: &PlayCampaign) -> Result<bool, ()> {\n        let exists = self.sql(&format!(\n            \"SELECT 1 FROM play_campaigns WHERE id = {};\",\n            sql_text(&campaign.id)\n        ))?;\n        if !exists.trim().is_empty() {\n            return Ok(false);\n        }\n        self.sql(&format!(\n            \"INSERT INTO play_campaigns(id,name,owner,max_players) VALUES ({},{},{},{});\",\n            sql_text(&campaign.id),\n            sql_text(&campaign.name),\n            sql_text(&campaign.owner),\n            campaign.max_players\n        ))\n        .map(|_| true)\n    }\n\n    fn update_play_campaign_document(\n        let storage = Self {\n            path: path.to_string(),\n        };\n        storage\n            .create_schema()\n            .map_err(|_| std::io::Error::other(\"could not initialize SQLite storage\"))?;\n        Ok(storage)\n    }\n\n    fn create_schema(&self) ->
